STORY 1
Ayesha was walking to the mountain road early in the morning. The clouds were low and grey.
Ayesha felt curious and found a broken clock near the path.
"What is this?" said Ayesha. "I must show it to someone."
At the busy station, Ayesha met Ali and a wise grandmother.
Ali looked at a broken clock and said, "I have seen this before. It belongs to Noor."
Together they walked back and returned it. Sara smiled and felt calm.
Ayesha learned one thing that day: Patience is better than hurry.

STORY 2
Hamza was walking to the busy station late in the evening. The clouds were low and grey.
Hamza felt happy and carried a torn letter near the path.
"What is this?" said Hamza. "I must show it to someone."
At the school garden, Hamza met Ayesha and a kind shopkeeper.
Ayesha looked at a torn letter and said, "I have seen this before. It belongs to Imran."
Together they walked back and returned it. Usman smiled and felt proud.
Hamza learned one thing that day: Patience is better than hurry.

STORY 3

Zainab learned one thing that day: A lost thing often leads to a new friend.

Imran felt tired and noticed a warm blanket near the path.
"What is this?" said Imran. "I must show it to someone."
At the school garden, Imran met Usman and a quiet neighbor.
Usman looked at a warm blanket and said, "I have seen this before. It belongs to Daniyal."
Together they walked back and returned it. Bilal smiled and felt happy.
Imran learned one thing that day: Patience is better than hurry.

Usman felt tired and carried a torn letter near the path.
"What is this?" said Usman. "I must show it to someone."
At the city park, Usman met Hamza and a young boy.
Hamza looked at a torn letter and said, "I have seen this before. It belongs to Hira."
Together they walked back and returned it. Kamran smiled and felt curious.
Usman learned one thing that day: Hard work always finds its way.

"What is this?" said Noor. "I must show it to someone."
At the city park, Noor met Imran and an old teacher.
Imran looked at a torn letter and said, "I have seen this before. It belongs to Hira."
Together they walked back and returned it. Daniyal smiled and felt happy.
Noor learned one thing that day: Hard work always finds its way.
